The benchmark standardizes the prediction task and the training and testing data. It also provides a unified evaluation protocol that supports direct comparison across models. Using this framework, we provide the first comprehensive comparative evaluation of non-learning, statistical learning, and deep learning models. We show that learning-based methods clearly outperform non-learning models, with graph neural networks achieving the best mean performance, while the strongest learning-based models remain relatively close to one another. Beyond aggregate mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE), the framework also provides breakdowns by prediction horizon and delay change, enabling more detailed analysis of model behavior across forecasting regimes.

</details>


### [124] [Identifying Gems from Roman RAPIDly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05103)
*Karan Gandhi,Ashish A. Mahabal,Jacob E. Jencson,Russ R. Laher,Ben Rusholme,Lin Yan,Ryan M. Lau,Schuyler D. Van Dyk,Mansi M. Kasliwal*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 针对罗曼望远镜无真实数据挑战，提出基于机器学习的RuBR模型用于区分真实与虚假瞬变天体，并通过领域适应策略为早期观测提供可靠分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罗曼望远镜将于2026年发射，需自动预警管道处理大量瞬变天体，但缺乏真实数据，故需开发鲁棒的真实-虚假分类方法。

Method: 提出RuBR模型及通用方法，包含三种变体：RuBR_comb（联合训练本地注入与OpenUniverse2024数据）、RuBR_loc（仅本地训练并测试于OpenUniverse2024）、RuBR_DA（领域适应模式混合部分数据训练）。

Result: 实验证明该方法在图像差分管道持续改进下，能有效实现真实-虚假分类，为罗曼任务早期无标签数据提供适应策略。

Conclusion: RuBR模型为罗曼望远镜自动（瞬变天体）发掘奠定基础，未来可适应真实观测场景。

Abstract: The Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope (Roman), set for launch as early as September 2026, will conduct wide-field infrared imaging surveys with unprecedented spatial resolution and cadence, enabling the discovery of millions of astronomical transients. Hence, it is necessary to have automated pipelines for generating alerts in place so that the telescope can begin discovering reliable transients and variable objects soon after it is launched. However, no real Roman data currently exist, making the development of such pipelines difficult. In this work, we present a machine learning model $RuBR$ and a general methodology for distinguishing genuine transient and variable detections from spurious (bogus) detections within the RAPID pipeline. In particular, we present three models using this methodology: $RuBR_{comb}$ trained and tested on combined locally injected and OpenUniverse2024 transients, $RuBR_{loc}$ trained on locally injected transients and tested on OpenUniverse2024 transients, and $RuBR_{DA}$ that combines locally injected transients with a fraction of OpenUniverse2024 transients in domain-adaptation mode for training. This paves the way for strategies to adapt the $RuBR_{comb}$ model to real observations in the absence of any ground-truth labels during the early phases of the Roman mission. While the image differencing pipeline continues to be improved, our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and its promise for robust real-bogus classification in the Roman era.

</details>


### [125] [RePercENT: Scaling Disentangled Representation Learning Beyond Two Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05109)
*Vasiliki Rizou,Pascal Frossard,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出RePercENT，一种自监督框架，通过即插即用架构实现超过两个模态的可扩展成对解耦，无需联合预训练，并提供理论最优性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态解耦方法受限于两模态场景，缺乏可扩展性，无法充分利用多模态数据中的共享和独特信息。

Method: 采用多模态即插即用架构，直接处理预提取的嵌入，引入联合优化目标同时推导共享和独特分量，并提供理论最优性保证。

Result: 在多种模态和任务中成功恢复解耦分量，保持竞争性能并显著降低计算复杂度。

Conclusion: RePercENT有效克服了多模态解耦的可扩展性瓶颈，为多模态表示学习提供了高效且理论支持的解决方案。

Abstract: To leverage the full potential of multimodal data, we need representations that go beyond the state-of-the-art alignment and fusion approaches and exploit all cross-modal interactions without sacrificing modality-specific information. Learning disentangled representations is a principled way to identify these underlying shared and unique factors that are hidden in observational data. However, while multimodal disentanglement is a compelling paradigm, existing methods are largely confined to the two-modality regime due to its inherent scalability bottleneck. To address this, we propose RePercENT, a self-supervised framework designed to surpass these limitations and unlocks scalable pairwise disentanglement beyond two modalities. Through a multimodal `plug-and-play' architecture, our approach operates directly on pre-extracted embeddings, eliminating the need for extensive joint pre-training while making no assumptions regarding the underlying modalities or foundation model backbones. Moreover, we introduce a joint optimization objective for simultaneously deriving the shared and unique components, and provide formal theoretical guarantees that characterize the optimality of our solution. Across diverse modalities and tasks, RePercENT successfully recovers disentangled components while maintaining competitive performance and significantly reducing computational complexity.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [MimeLens: Position-Agnostic Content-Type Detection for Binary Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04171)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MimeLens，一种在均匀随机偏移处预训练的小型BERT风格编码器，无需文件头即可从任意位置对125种MIME类型进行分类，在完整文件头部准确率比Magika提升10.7pp，并能处理Magika无法处理的中流数据，但CPU延迟高一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的文件类型分类系统（如Google Magika）假设从已知偏移量获取完整文件，无法处理实际任务中出现的单个数据包载荷、无头碎片、随机磁盘块或分块上传等输入。

Method: 采用小BERT风格编码器，在训练时从每个文件内均匀随机偏移位置采样二进制内容，去除文件头位置特权，提供标准上下文和短上下文两种变体；输入任意位置的字节块，输出libmagic的125种MIME标签。

Result: 在完整文件头部数据上，top-1准确率比Magika v1.1高10.7个百分点；在单个中流UDP数据包和随机中文件磁盘块上，分类准确率是libmagic和Magika的两倍以上；CPU延迟比Magika慢1-2个数量级，但消费级GPU或批量处理时相当。

Conclusion: MimeLens在文件类型分类任务上优于现有方法，特别是在非标准输入场景下，代价是更高的CPU延迟，但可通过GPU或批处理缓解；所有训练检查点已发布在Hugging Face。

Abstract: File-type classification underlies many workflows like malware triage, forensic carving, packet inspection, and storage indexing. Learned systems such as Google's Magika assume whole-file access at a known offset, so they break on the inputs many of these tasks actually produce, like a single packet payload, a header-less carved fragment, a random disk block, or a chunked upload. We introduce MimeLens, a family of small BERT-style encoders pretrained on binary content from windows sampled at a uniformly random offset within each file, with no privileged head-of-file position, in standard- and short-context variants. A byte chunk goes in from anywhere in a file, no header needed and no fixed size; out comes one of libmagic's 125 MIME labels. On the clean head of complete files, MimeLens beats Magika v1.1 by +10.7 pp top-1 on libmagic-labeled data, and it keeps classifying where Magika cannot: from a single mid-stream UDP packet, and more than twice as accurately as libmagic and Magika on random mid-file disk blocks. The cost is latency: MimeLens runs roughly one to two orders of magnitude slower per sample on CPU than Magika, though it matches on consumer GPUs or in batch. All trained checkpoints are released on Hugging Face (mjbommar/mimelens-001-*).

</details>


### [127] [Formal verification of the S-two AIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04311)
*Jeremy Avigad,Anat Ganor,Lior Goldberg,David Levit,Ohad Nir,Yoav Seginer,Alon Titelman*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 使用Lean 4证明助手验证StarkWare S-two证明器中Cairo虚拟机的代数中间表示(AIR)编码的正确性，确保AIR可满足性蕴含计算声明的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保区块链上Cairo程序执行声明的可靠性，通过形式化验证AIR编码的合理性来避免因编码错误导致的安全漏洞。

Method: 利用Lean 4定理证明器，对Cairo语言的AIR约束系统进行形式化建模，证明其可满足性蕴含程序执行完成的语义正确性。

Result: 成功验证了AIR编码与Cairo虚拟机语义之间的逻辑等价性，确认circle STARK证明系统的可靠性基础。

Conclusion: 形式化验证确保了零知识证明系统中计算声明的编码正确性，为区块链应用提供了数学保障。

Abstract: StarkWare's S-two prover provides an efficient means for establishing, on blockchain, that a program written in the Cairo virtual machine language runs to completion. The latter claim is encoded by an algebraic intermediate representation (AIR) that captures the semantics of the Cairo language. The AIR asserts the existence of tables of values from a finite field satisfying certain algebraic constraints. A cryptographic interactive proof system, circle STARK, provides an efficiently-checked certificate that the AIR is satisfied. We describe our verification, using the Lean 4 proof assistant, that the AIR encoding is sound, which is to say, the satisfiability of the AIR implies the computational claim.

</details>


### [128] [Inverse Critical Experiment Design via Gradient Optimization and a Multigroup Attention-Based Neural Network Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04033)
*Will Savage,Logan Burnett,Dean Price*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度神经网络代理模型和非参数梯度优化的临界实验逆设计方法，通过最大化中子学相似度系数$c_k$来生成实验几何，并成功应用于HALEU燃料运输容器的验证，$c_k$最高达0.97757。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临界实验对先进核反应堆和燃料概念的验证覆盖不足，需要高中子学相似度的实验设计来降低核数据不确定性带来的偏差。

Method: 使用U-Net卷积编码器-解码器结合多组注意力池化层的深度神经网络，基于OpenMC计算的灵敏度向量进行训练，并通过梯度优化直接改变材料网格分配以最大化$c_k$。

Result: 对TN-LC运输容器的三个配置优化后，分别达到0.97757、0.81324和0.93276的$c_k$值，优于传统池化方法且内部行为可解释。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The validation of advanced nuclear reactor designs and fuel concepts requires critical experiments with high neutronic similarity to the target technology. Neutronic similarity is quantified by the correlation coefficient $c_k$, which captures the shared bias in $k_\text{eff}$ induced by uncertainties in nuclear data. Generally, a $c_k\geq0.9$ is needed for an experiment to be sufficiently similar to a target technology. This work presents a methodology for the inverse design of critical experiments. Deep neural network surrogate modeling and nonparametric gradient optimization are used to generate experiment geometries that maximize $c_k$.
  A deep neural network is trained on OpenMC-calculated sensitivity vectors for grid-based critical experiment geometries. The model architecture combines a U-Net convolutional encoder-decoder with a novel multigroup attention pooling layer, introduced to capture the differing spatial dependencies of sensitivities. Multigroup attention pooling is shown to achieve better performance than traditional pooling, as well as interpretable internal behavior. The differentiability of the surrogate enables gradient-based optimization of the full combinatorial design space, allowing $c_k$ to be maximized by directly changing the material assignment of each position in the geometry grid.
  The method is applied to the validation of the TN-Americas TN-LC transportation cask with HALEU fuel, for which existing critical experiment coverage is limited. The optimization procedure is shown to produce experiment geometries achieving $c_k$ scores of 0.97757, 0.81324, and 0.93276 for three configurations of interest. This approach demonstrates the potential of deep learning and gradient optimization to accelerate the development of advanced nuclear technology.

</details>


### [129] [Online Skill Learning for Web Agents via State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04391)
*Jiaxi Li,Ke Deng,Yun Wang,Jingyuan Huang,Yucheng Shi,Qiaoyu Tan,Jin Lu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出状态引导的动态检索（SGDR）方法，在Web自动化中实现步骤级的技能复用，显著提升多步任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线技能学习方法仅在任务级别静态复用技能，忽略了执行过程中网页状态的动态变化，导致初始技能无法覆盖后续状态。

Method: SGDR包含三个组件：滑动窗口提取子流程、双文本-代码表示连接检索与执行、状态引导动态检索机制匹配技能与当前状态。

Result: 在WebArena五个领域上，SGDR平均成功率GPT-4.1达37.5%、Qwen3-4B达24.3%，相对最强基线分别提升10.6%和10.0%。

Conclusion: SGDR通过动态状态匹配实现了有效的步骤级技能复用，显著提升了Web智能体的自动化性能。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly rely on reusable skills to improve multi-step web automation across related tasks. A growing line of work studies online skill learning, where agents continually induce skills from previous task trajectories and reuse them in future tasks on the fly. However, existing methods mainly reuse skills at the task-level: a fixed set of skills is retrieved based on the initial task instruction and then held fixed throughout execution. This static strategy is misaligned with web execution, where the appropriate next action depends not only on the task goal but also on the current webpage state, which often transitions into situations that the initial skills fail to cover. To address this gap, we propose State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval (SGDR), an online skill learning method that enables stepwise skill reuse for web agents. SGDR consists of three components: a sliding-window extraction process that turns completed trajectories into reusable sub-procedures invokable at intermediate execution states, a dual text-code representation that connects skill retrieval with executable action, and a state-grounded dynamic retrieval mechanism that matches skills to both the task goal and the current webpage state. Experiments on WebArena across five domains show that SGDR consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving average success rates of 37.5% with GPT-4.1 and 24.3% with Qwen3-4B, corresponding to relative gains of 10.6% and 10.0% over the strongest baseline, respectively. The code is available at https://github.com/plusnli/skill-dynamic-retrieval.

</details>


### [130] [TeleHunt: A Framework and Tool for Efficient Cybercriminal Community Discovery on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04657)
*Roy Ricaldi,Victor Asanache,Luca Allodi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出TeleHunt框架，系统评估在Telegram上发现网络犯罪社区的不同策略，使用参考驱动雪球采样方法，基于172M消息数据集分析种子来源、指针类型和探索策略对发现效率、可访问性和重新发现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram成为网络犯罪活动的重要平台，但缺乏系统的方法来评估不同发现策略的有效性，现有研究未比较种子来源、指针类型和探索策略对发现结果的影响。

Method: 构建TeleHunt框架，采用参考驱动雪球采样策略，集成消息级分类、上下文过滤和市场细分标签，利用开放和暗网种子，系统评估三个维度（效率、可访问性、重新发现）。

Result: 提供了模块化网络犯罪内容发现流水线、首次Telegram发现策略系统比较及市场细分可访问性实证特征、标注数据集（172M+消息，6,022个社区）。

Conclusion: 不同发现策略在效率、可访问性和重新发现上存在显著差异，参考驱动雪球采样结合上下文过滤能有效发现网络犯罪社区，数据集为后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: This paper presents TeleHunt, a framework and tool for evaluating the effectiveness of different strategies to discover cybercriminal communities on Telegram. TeleHunt employs a set of reference-driven snowballing strategies, integrating message-level classification, contextual filtering, and market-segment labeling. Using open- and dark-web seeds, we systematically evaluate how seed source, pointer type, and exploration strategy influence discovery outcomes in three dimensions: efficiency, accessibility, and rediscovery. Our work provides (i) a modular cybercrime content discovery pipeline, (ii) the first systematic comparison of Telegram discovery strategies with an empirical characterization of market-segment accessibility, and (iii) a labeled dataset of over 172 million messages from 6,022 Telegram communities.

</details>


### [131] [Variance Reduction for Heavy-Tailed Monetization Metrics in Ranking Experiments via Post-Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04110)
*Neeti Pokharna,Olivier Jeunen,Yatharth Saraf,Aleksei Ustimenko*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出结合后分层与CUPED的方差缩减框架，在排名/推荐系统A/B实验中减少约45%流量需求的同时保持统计效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评估中货币化指标呈重尾分布，少数用户主导均值和方差，导致小流量下统计功效低、结论不可靠。

Method: 利用实验前协变量，通过后分层（post-stratification）与CUPED（对照实验前协变量）组合方法实现方差缩减。

Result: 在ShareChat的排名驱动货币化实验中，该方法显著降低方差，以标准指标约45%的流量达到等效统计置信度。

Conclusion: 提供了实际设计选择、护栏和局限性，为信息检索与推荐系统中后分层的适用场景给出指导。

Abstract: Online evaluation of ranking and retrieval systems often relies on downstream monetization metrics such as app revenue or creator earnings. These metrics are typically heavy-tailed, with a small fraction of users dominating both mean and variance, leading to low statistical power and unreliable conclusions in A/B experiments -- especially under limited traffic.
  We present a practical framework for variance reduction in online experiments by combining post-stratification with CUPED. Our approach leverages pre-experiment covariates to improve the sensitivity of monetization experiments without requiring additional traffic. Deployed at ShareChat across ranking-driven monetization experiments, the method substantially reduces variance and improves decision stability, achieving equivalent statistical confidence with ~45\% less traffic than standard metrics. We further discuss practical design choices, guardrails, and limitations, providing guidance on when post-stratification is appropriate for real-world information retrieval and Recommendation systems.

</details>


### [132] [Low-rank Distributional Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04176)
*Jiayi Wang,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布矩阵补全方法，利用核均值嵌入和Tucker秩对分布型矩阵进行低秩建模，并引入功能展开算子连接无限维结构与有限维张量秩，从而设计出新型估计器，给出非渐近误差界并通过实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵补全处理标量值，但实际中条目可能为概率分布，且观测有限样本而非直接分布。现有方法无法处理这种分布型矩阵的低秩结构与无限维嵌入挑战。

Method: 采用核均值嵌入表示分布，定义分布值矩阵的Tucker秩，引入功能展开算子将分布低秩结构映射到经典张量Tucker秩，据此构建新型估计器。

Result: 推导了估计器的非渐近误差界，理论刻画统计性能，合成数据与真实应用实验表明方法有效。

Conclusion: 所提分布矩阵补全方法在理论和实证上均表现良好，为分布型高维数据建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study a distributional generalization of the matrix completion problem in which each entry of the target matrix is a probability distribution rather than a scalar. In this setting, only a subset of matrix entries is observed, and even for observed entries, the underlying distributions are not directly accessible; instead, we observe finitely many samples drawn from them. To represent distributional entries, we employ kernel mean embeddings and introduce a notion of Tucker rank for distribution-valued matrices to capture their low-rank structure. The infinite-dimensional nature of kernel embeddings poses significant methodological challenges. To address this, we introduce functional unfolding operators that link the proposed distributional low-rank structure to the classical Tucker rank for finite-dimensional tensors. Based on this framework, we propose a novel estimator for distributional matrix completion. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds that characterize the statistical performance of the estimator. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [133] [BiNSGPS: Geometry Problem Solving via Bidirectional Neuro-Symbolic Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04648)
*Qi Wang,Peijie Wang,Fei Yin,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出双向神经符号交互框架BiNSGPS，通过MLLM与符号求解器的双向反馈动态修正形式化表示，解决几何问题求解中单向管线的脆性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号混合方法采用单向管线（神经输出→符号求解），缺乏反馈机制导致早期错误传播，亟需突破双向交互瓶颈以提升系统鲁棒性。

Method: 构建MLLM Adviser与符号求解器的双向交互：求解器向Adviser反馈不一致表征或推理冲突，Adviser据此动态修正形式化表示或生成辅助假设。

Result: 通过双向交互机制有效解决符号冲突，支持复杂推理链，减少早期错误累积。

Conclusion: BiNSGPS框架通过双向神经符号交互克服了单向管线脆弱性，在几何问题求解中提升适应性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Geometry problem solving poses distinct challenges in artificial intelligence. Existing approaches typically fall into two paradigms: symbolic methods, which exhibit limited adaptability, and neural methods, which are prone to hallucinations. Recent neuro-symbolic hybrids predominantly rely on a unidirectional pipeline where neural outputs are fed into solvers without feedback, making system brittle to early-stage errors. To break this unidirectional bottleneck, we propose BiNSGPS, a framework that establishes Bidirectional Neuro-Symbolic Interaction (BiNS) between a MLLM Adviser and a Symbolic Solver. MLLM Adviser actively incorporates feedback from the symbolic solver to dynamically rectify inconsistent formal representations or propose auxiliary hypotheses, resolving symbolic conflicts and facilitating complex deductions.

</details>


### [134] [KODA: Contrastive Representation Comparison and Alignment for Vision-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04180)
*Youqi Wu,Mohammad Jalali,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出KODA框架，通过核方法对比分析视觉-语言基础模型（如CLIP、SigLIP）表征的结构差异，识别并利用弱聚类样本子集进行表征对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型评估依赖下游任务性能，未能解释表征间的结构性差异，需一种可解释的对比分析方法。

Method: 基于核优化的对比嵌入聚类框架KODA：通过模态核组合构建统一多模态核，将差异发现建模为约束优化问题（增强目标表征聚类、抑制参考表征聚类），并采用随机傅里叶特征实现大规模数据集的可扩展性。

Result: 在视觉-语言表征上识别出一致且可解释的差异结构，并提供用于表征对齐的样本子集。

Conclusion: KODA能有效揭示和利用视觉-语言模型表征的结构性差异，为表征对齐提供可解释工具。

Abstract: Vision-language foundation models such as CLIP and SigLIP provide widely used representations for multimodal learning systems. While these models are typically compared through downstream performance, such evaluations often do not explain how their representations differ structurally. In this work, we study this problem through the task of Contrastive Embedding Clustering: identifying sample subsets that are weakly clustered under one representation but strongly clustered under another. We propose \emph{Kernel Optimization for Discrepancy Analysis (KODA)}, a kernel-based framework for contrastive representation comparison and alignment. KODA constructs unified multimodal kernels through modality-wise kernel composition and formulates discrepancy discovery as a constrained optimization problem that searches for coherent structures in one representation while suppressing coherence in a reference representation. This yields interpretable discrepancy directions associated with specific sample subsets and modality interactions. To scale KODA to large vision-language datasets, we develop randomized low-dimensional approximations of joint kernels using random projections, including Random Fourier Features for shift-invariant kernels. Empirically, KODA identifies consistent and interpretable discrepancy structures across vision-language representations and provides sample subsets for representation alignment. The code is available at https://github.com/yokiwuuu/KODA.

</details>


### [135] [Metric-Aware Hybrid Forecasting for the CTF4Science Lorenz Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04191)
*Cen Lu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种指标感知的混合系统，针对CTF4Science Lorenz挑战中的不同指标族分配不同预测器，在公共排行榜上取得83.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一模型无法在所有指标上占优，需根据指标特性组合多种预测方法。

Method: 混合系统包含三部分：合成预训练去噪器用于轨迹重建、Lorenz ODE拟合和轨迹摄动用于前20步预测、直方图尾部替换用于长时分布匹配。

Result: 中间系统得分83.83551，最终版本83.85529。

Conclusion: 针对不同指标族设计专用预测器是提升多任务基准性能的有效策略。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the CTF4Science Lorenz challenge, a benchmark that mixes short-horizon forecasting, long-time distribution matching, and trajectory reconstruction across nine task pairs. The key discovery is that no single model family dominated all metrics. Instead, we built a metric-aware hybrid system that assigned a different predictor to each metric family: (1) synthetic-pretrained denoisers for full-trajectory reconstruction, (2) Lorenz ODE fitting and trajectory shooting for the first 20 forecast steps, and (3) histogram-tail substitution using synthetic Lorenz libraries for long-time evaluation. A representative mature submission from this system family scored 83.83551 on the public leaderboard, and a small follow-up stack of the same ideas reached 83.85529. We focus on the cleaner intermediate system because it captures the full method while remaining simple enough to reproduce and analyze, while the final submission can be understood as a conservative extension of the same backbone.

</details>


### [136] [Derivative Informed Learning of Exchange-Correlation Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04279)
*Eike S. Eberhard,Luca A. Thiede,Abdul Aldossary,Andreas Burger,Nicholas Gao,Vignesh Bhethanabotla,Alán Aspuru-Guzik,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出导数信息损失（DI-Loss）来训练机器学习交换相关泛函，通过监督能量的一阶和二阶导数，在四种架构上使总能量MAE平均降低66%，并将杂化泛函SCF迭代次数减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习交换相关泛函虽能直接学习参考数据，但性能仍不如传统O(N^4)杂化泛函，需要更有效的训练方法。

Method: 在杂化蒸馏框架下，使用导数信息损失（DI-Loss）在Grassmann流形上监督能量对密度矩阵的导数，对齐学习泛函与目标泛函的局部响应。

Result: 总能量MAE平均降低66%，平均场能量误差从1.2降至0.8 mEh，杂化泛函SCF迭代次数减少50%，激发能MAE降低19-35%，但偶极矩和L2密度误差未一致改善。

Conclusion: DI-Loss通过导数监督有效提升了机器学习泛函的精度和计算效率，但密度相关指标仍需改进。

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) exchange-correlation (XC) functionals aim to replace human-designed density functional approximations by learning directly from reference data, but they still do not consistently outperform traditional $\mathcal{O}(N^4)$-scaling hybrid functionals. We study a hybrid-distillation setting in which $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$-scaling ML-XC functionals are trained to reproduce B3LYP/def2-SVP targets. We introduce Derivative Informed XC-Loss (DI-Loss), a loss that incorporates additional information from the reference hybrid functional by supervising first and second derivatives of the energy on the Grassmannian of admissible density matrices. Rather than only matching the self-consistent fixed point, DI-Loss aligns the local first- and second-order response of the learned functional with that of the target functional. Across four evaluated architectures, DI-Loss consistently improves the main energy metrics. Averaged uniformly across architectures, the total-energy MAE decreases by 66% relative to energy and density supervision alone. The density-sensitive mean-field energy metric $E_ρ$ improves from $1.2$ to $0.8$ mEh on average, while dipole and $\mathcal{L}_2$ density errors do not improve uniformly. We further show that densities from the distilled functionals reduce hybrid-functional SCF iterations by up to 50%. In downstream TDDFT calculations, Hessian supervision improves excited-state predictions, with XCdiff reducing the mean excitation-energy MAE by 19 - 35%.

</details>


### [137] [Strabo: Declarative Specification and Implementation of Agentic Interaction Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05043)
*Samuel H. Christie,Amit K. Chopra,Munindar P. Singh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Strabo通过将UCP的结账部分建模为声明式Langshaw协议，并使用Peach实现智能体，展示了声明式交互协议在Agentic AI中的优势及与现有系统的互操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动声明式多智能体系统从学术研究走向工业实践，为现有Agentic AI系统（如Google的UCP）引入形式化方法提供渐进式路径。

Method: 将UCP结账流程建模为声明式Langshaw协议，并利用Peach编程模型实现智能体，随后验证其与Google实现的UCP智能体的互操作性。

Result: 成功建模UCP结账部分，Peach智能体与Google的UCP智能体实现互操作，验证了声明式方法的保真性。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The last few years have witnessed major advances in the modeling and implementation of multiagent systems based on declarative interaction protocols. Our contribution, Strabo, establishes the relevance of these advances to ongoing industry efforts in Agentic AI. Specifically, we consider UCP, the Universal Commerce Protocol, a recent Google-led effort to standardize e-commerce interactions for AI agents. Our exercise is in two parts. One, we model the part of UCP dealing with checkouts as a declarative Langshaw protocol and implement agents using Peach, a programming model for Langshaw. This part of the exercise brings out the advantages of formal, declarative specifications. Two, we show that Peach agents can interoperate with UCP agents implemented by Google, thereby establishing the fidelity of our approach with respect to UCP. Such interoperation enables the incremental introduction of declarative protocols and agents into a conventional setting, indicating a pathway by which EMAS ideas could influence practice without demanding a wholesale update.

</details>


### [138] [AutoLab: Can Frontier Models Solve Long-Horizon Auto Research and Engineering Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05080)
*Zhangchen Xu,Junda Chen,Yue Huang,Dongfu Jiang,Jiefeng Chen,Hang Hua,Zijian Wu,Zheyuan Liu,Zexue He,Lichi Li,Shizhe Diao,Jiaxin Pei,Jinsung Yoon,Hao Zhang,Mengdi Wang,Radha Poovendran,Misha Sra,Alex Pentland,Zichen Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: AutoLab是一个用于评估超长时域闭环优化的基准，包含36个专家设计的任务，测试模型在持续迭代改进中的能力，结果表明持久性比初始尝试质量更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估单轮或短时域任务，无法捕捉长时间跨度内的持续迭代优化挑战。

Method: 构建AutoLab基准，包含系统优化、谜题挑战、模型开发和CUDA核优化四个领域的36个任务，每个任务提供次优基线，要求代理在固定墙钟时间内改进，并评估17个前沿模型。

Result: 成功的关键预测因素是持久性（反复基准测试、编辑和整合反馈），而非初始尝试质量；Claude-opus-4.6表现最佳，但多数前沿模型过早终止或在预算内进展甚微。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Scientific and engineering progress is fundamentally a long-horizon iterative process: proposing changes, running experiments, measuring outcomes, and continuously refining artifacts. Yet existing benchmarks for frontier models primarily evaluate either single-turn responses or short-horizon agent trajectories, failing to capture the challenges of sustained iterative improvement over extended time horizons. To address this gap, we introduce AutoLab, a new benchmark for ultra long-horizon closed-loop optimization. AutoLab consists of 36 realistic, expert-curated tasks spanning four diverse domains: system optimization, puzzle & challenge, model development, and CUDA kernel optimization. Each task begins with a correct but deliberately suboptimal baseline and challenges agents to improve it within a strict wall-clock budget. Evaluating 17 state-of-the-art models reveals the dominant predictor of success is not the quality of an agent's initial attempt, but its persistence in repeatedly benchmarking, editing, and incorporating empirical feedback. While claude-opus-4.6 exhibits strong long-horizon optimization capabilities, most frontier models, including several proprietary ones, either terminate prematurely or exhaust their budgets with minimal progress. These results underscore the importance of time awareness and persistent iteration in autonomous agents. We open-source the full benchmark, evaluation harness, and task artifacts, to accelerate research toward truly capable long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [139] [Knowledge Index of Noah's Ark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05104)
*Sheng Jin,Minghao Liu,Yunze Xiao,Zeqi Zhou,Heli Qi,Yifan Yao,Meishu Song,Kaijing Ma,Xuan Zhang,Sicong Jiang,Yizhe Li,Ningshan Ma,Jie Wei,Ziniu Li,Minglai Yang,Bangya Liu,Yiming Liang,Xiao Fang,Qingcheng Zeng,Jiarui Liu,Rui Yang,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang,Jiaheng Liu,Zihan Wang,Weihao Xuan,Ge Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出KINA基准测试，涵盖261个细粒度学科共899项，通过覆盖目标实现学科代表性，并证明锦标赛奖励机制优于固定报酬，42个模型评测显示性能分层显著，工具增强带来差异化的分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM知识基准存在三大问题：基于规模扩展的设计缺乏学科代表性、固定报酬导致标注者懒惰共识、有限测试预算下排名不稳定未审计。

Method: 将代表性建模为专家锚点的覆盖目标，通过贪心近似（1-1/e）保证；设计锦标赛奖励机制并证明其弱FOSD支配固定报酬；采用自助法计算排名稳定性统计。

Result: 顶级模型Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview得分53.17%，领先；模型性能呈三层结构（前沿>48%，强模型38-45%，低性能略高于10%基线）；工具增强最多提升5.17分。

Conclusion: KINA通过学科覆盖性设计和激励机制优化提升了基准可靠性，揭示当前模型能力天花板接近50%，且排名受测试预算影响需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Knowledge benchmarks for LLMs face three issues: scaling-driven designs that do not operationalize disciplinary representativeness; flat-payment annotation that permits lazy consensus; and unaudited ranking instability under bounded test budgets. We introduce KINA, an 899-item benchmark across 261 fine-grained disciplines, with two formal results. First, we cast representativeness as a coverage-style objective over expert-elicited anchors and operationalize disciplinary representativeness through a proxy, yielding a (1-1/e) greedy approximation (Proposition 1); the guarantee applies to the proxy, not to population representativeness. Second, we prove a bonus-on-bar tournament weakly FOSD-dominates flat payment in released-review quality, with incentive-compatibility threshold B > Delta C / Delta p_min (Theorem 1). Evaluating 42 models from 13 labs, the top model, Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview, reaches 53.17%, followed by Claude-Opus-4.6 at 49.92% and GPT-5.4 at 48.55%, leaving substantial headroom below saturation. The full leaderboard shows a tiered structure rather than a smooth total order: a small frontier tier lies above 48%, a dense strong-model tier spans roughly 38-45%, and low-performing models remain only modestly above the 10% chance baseline. Tool augmentation adds up to 5.17 points across the five tool-use evaluations, with gains varying substantially across models. We report bootstrap ranking-stability statistics to make bounded-budget variance explicit and to discourage over-interpretation of adjacent ranks.

</details>


### [140] [Offline-to-Online Learning in Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04305)
*Kushagra Chandak,Toshinori Kitamura,Xiaoqi Tan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究随机线性bandit中额外离线数据的使用，提出一种平衡离线与在线数据的算法，在理论上证明了其同时优于纯在线和纯离线方案，并通过实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中在线学习经常伴有离线数据集，但结构化环境中离线到在线的权衡尚不明确。

Method: 提出一种线性bandit算法，初期依赖离线数据，后期逐步增加在线探索以平衡两者。

Result: 算法在在线交互次数上对最优动作取得次线性遗憾，且离线样本增多时离线参考的遗憾下降。

Conclusion: 该方法能同时与纯在线和纯离线方案竞争，实验证明其在多种参数下有效。

Abstract: We study online learning with an additional offline dataset in the stochastic linear bandit setting. Although this problem arises frequently in practice, the offline-to-online tradeoff remains poorly understood in structured environments. We propose a linear bandit algorithm that balances this tradeoff: it relies on offline data during early rounds, and increasingly favors exploration as the horizon grows. We establish regret bounds showing that our method is simultaneously competitive with both purely online and purely offline solutions. In particular, it achieves sublinear regret relative to the optimal action in the number of online interactions, while its regret relative to an offline reference decreases as the number of offline samples grows. Empirical results further demonstrate its effectiveness across various problem parameters.

</details>


### [141] [A Geometric Characterization of the Stationary Plateau for Two-Layer Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04327)
*Tian Ding,Dawei Li,Ruoyu Sun*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 研究了两层神经网络损失景观中静止高原的几何结构，重点分析了神经元分裂现象，并基于内Hessian矩阵分类了高原上的所有静止点，揭示了分裂系数与局部几何的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络宽度扩展时静止点性质的保持或变化，为模型扩展和重参数化提供几何洞见。

Method: 通过定义每神经元的“内Hessian”矩阵，分析其正定性并结合分裂系数，分类高原上的局部极小点和鞍点。

Result: 分裂局部极小点可能产生局部极小与鞍点的混合或全鞍点高原，而分裂鞍点总是产生鞍点高原；在内Hessian负定等温和假设下，能识别确定鞍点区域。

Conclusion: 统一并扩展了先前景观分析，揭示了宽度扩展如何影响静止点性质，为理解神经网络重参数化提供了新几何视角。

Abstract: We investigate the geometric structure of stationary plateaus that arise in the loss landscape of two-layer neural networks with smooth activation functions. We focus on the phenomenon of "neuron splitting" where duplicating a hidden neuron yields an affine set of stationary points in a wider network. We provide a comprehensive classification of all stationary points on these plateaus, determining under what conditions they constitute local minima or saddle points. Our characterization hinges on a per-neuron curvature object we term the "inner Hessian" matrix. Our analysis reveals that the definiteness of the inner Hessian and the choice of splitting coefficients jointly dictate the local geometry of the plateau. We show that "splitting" a local minimum can yield either a mixture of local minima and saddles or an all-saddle plateau, with a concrete sure-saddle region identified under mild assumptions. In contrast, splitting a saddle point always produces a plateau of saddle points. Our results unify and extend prior landscape analyses, elucidating when and how model expansion preserves or alters the nature of stationary points. These findings offer new geometric insights into the effects of width expansion and reparameterization in neural networks.

</details>


### [142] [Literature-Guided Minimax Optimization of Virtual Epilepsy Neurostimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04339)
*Cathy Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种结合文献挖掘、虚拟脑模拟和大语言模型优化的最小化最大损失管线，用于癫痫神经调控的鲁棒参数设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型优化通常追求平均表现，但无法保证对最不耐受患者的疗效，需要更鲁棒的优化目标。

Method: 利用PubMed文献抽取假设、TVB模拟癫痫网络，大语言模型驱动黑盒优化，目标函数最大化最差病例的负发作方差异。

Result: 内源性参数优化使最差奖励提高39.8%；外源性刺激优化仅提高1.7%，且20人虚拟队列无统计显著改善。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Computational models of epilepsy promise patient-specific treatment design, but most optimization workflows still search for parameters that perform well on average. In neuromodulation, this is a weak target: a protocol that improves the mean response can still fail in the patient whose network is least tolerant to stimulation. We present a literature-guided minimax pipeline that couples PubMed-scale hypothesis extraction, The Virtual Brain (TVB) Epileptor simulations, and large-language-model-guided black-box optimization. The optimizer proposes either intrinsic model-control parameters or clinically interpretable external-stimulation protocols; TVB evaluates each proposal across sampled virtual patients; and the objective maximizes worst-case reward, defined as the negative variance of simulated seizure activity. In the intrinsic model-control experiment, the best archived parameter set improved worst-case reward from -0.5285 to -0.3182, a 39.8% gain over baseline. The clinical-style external-stimulation search produced a much smaller worst-case improvement (1.7%), and a 20-patient virtual cohort showed no aggregate benefit (p=0.9019), despite a 55% responder rate and a positive temporal-lobe subgroup signal. The study should be read as an in silico proof of concept for robust, literature-aware neurostimulation design, not as clinical evidence.

</details>


### [143] [MeshTok: Efficient Multi-Scale Tokenization for Scalable PDE Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04366)
*Yanshun Zhao,Xiaoyu Peng,Jiamin Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MeshTok框架，基于自适应网格细化的非均匀分块策略，在Transformer序列中处理多尺度令牌，提升复杂PDE求解的效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统patchified Transformers采用均匀空间分区，计算资源平均分配，无法高效表示具有局部变化特征的复杂PDE解。

Method: 受自适应网格细化启发，选择性细化具有尖锐梯度、瞬态特征或多尺度结构的区域，在固定模拟网格上生成异构多尺度令牌序列，无需特殊架构即可同时捕获全局上下文和局部细节。

Result: 在多个PDE族和基准数据集上，MeshTok相比均匀网格基线持续改善效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 自适应多尺度令牌化是神经PDE建模中可扩展且通用的设计原则。

Abstract: Conventional patchified Transformers operate on uniform spatial partitions, distributing computational effort evenly across the domain irrespective of local features. This inflexible tokenization scheme is inherently limited in its ability to efficiently represent and process solutions to complex PDEs. To address this, we propose MeshTok, an adaptive mesh refinement (AMR)-inspired tokenization and sequence modeling framework. This method selectively refines spatial regions exhibiting sharp gradients, transient features, or multiscale structures, generating a heterogeneous set of multiscale tokens defined on a fixed simulation grid. These tokens are processed within a unified Transformer sequence, enabling the model to simultaneously capture coarse-grained global context and fine-grained local details without requiring specialized architectural components. Although adaptive refinement moderately increases token count, it promotes a more targeted allocation of computational resources to physically informative regions, which we view as a practical inductive bias rather than a formal optimality guarantee. Experimental evaluations across multiple PDE families and benchmark datasets demonstrate that MeshTok consistently improves the efficiency-accuracy trade-off compared to uniform-grid baselines. This suggests adaptive multiscale tokenization as a scalable and generalizable design principle for neural PDE modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/SCAILab-USTC/MeshTok.

</details>


### [144] [An Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04408)
*Rui Zhang,Jinhang Liu,Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种融合差分进化与梯度下降优化的集成潜在因子模型，通过自适应性加权融合两种优化策略，提升高维不完全数据的表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在因子模型仅依赖梯度下降优化，在处理异质性高维不完全数据时易产生不足且有偏的表征。

Method: 分别用差分进化和梯度下降独立建模两个多样性潜在因子模型，再通过自适应性加权机制融合二者优势。

Result: 在三个高维不完全数据集上，ELFM-DEGDO的性能持续优于多个相关潜在因子模型。

Conclusion: 融合差分进化与梯度下降优化的集成策略能生成更全面、偏差更小的表征，有效提升模型性能。

Abstract: High-dimensional and incomplete (HDI) data are prevalent in many real-world big data scenarios. Latent factor models serve as a common representation learning approach, capable of uncovering informative latent factors from such data. Nevertheless, most existing latent factor models rely solely on gradient descent for optimization, which may lead to insufficient and biased representations, particularly when dealing with heterogeneous HDI data. Thus, this study proposes an Ensembled Latent Factor Model via Differential Evolution and Gradient Descent Optimization (ELFM-DEGDO) with two-fold designed: 1) two diverse latent factor models are independently modeled via differential evolution and gradient descent optimization, respectively, and 2) the two diverse latent factor models are combined via a customized self-adaptive weighting mechanism to effectively fuse their strengths. By leveraging the complementary advantages of both optimization paradigms, ELFM-DEGDO is able to produce more comprehensive and less biased representations for HDI data. Three HDI datasets are tested to show that ELFM-DEGDO consistently performs better than related several latent factor models.

</details>


### [145] [The price of multi-group transductive learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04423)
*Noah Bergam,Samuel Deng,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 转导学习中的多组学习器在组错误率上可能承受与组数线性相关的乘法惩罚，最多可达样本量的平方根，而统计可实现设置中惩罚独立于组数且仅对数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示转导设置下多组学习器相对于单组设置的固有性能劣势，并与统计设置形成对比。

Method: 通过理论分析比较转导和统计两种设置下多组学习器的错误率边界。

Result: 转导设置中惩罚与组数线性相关且随样本量平方根增长，统计设置中惩罚至多对数级。

Conclusion: 转导学习在多组场景中面临更严峻的误差惩罚，需要不同的算法设计策略。

Abstract: We show every multi-group learner in the transductive setting may incur a multiplicative penalty in its error rate on some group relative to the error rate achievable in the single-group setting, and the penalty can increasing linearly with the number of groups, up to roughly the square-root of the sample size. This stands in stark contrast to optimal multi-group learners in an analogous (group-realizable) statistical setting, where the penalty is always at most logarithmic in the sample size and independent of the number of groups.

</details>


### [146] [LoopMoE: Unifying Iterative Computation with Mixture-of-Experts for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04438)
*Wenkai Chen,Tianshu Li,Wenyong Huang,Yichun Yin,Lifeng Shang,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: LoopMoE 结合稀疏路由与循环权重共享，通过迭代自适应层归一化和容量平衡策略，在相同参数和计算量下超越传统 MoE 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环架构依赖稠密骨干网络，导致参数量与单 token 计算量无法解耦，难以在同等预算下评估迭代计算的效果。

Method: 提出 LoopMoE，包含两个核心设计：1) IterAdaLN，基于迭代索引和 token 隐状态联合调节权重共享的对称性；2) 容量平衡策略，恢复注意力与 FFN 激活参数比至非循环参考模型水平。

Result: 在 3B 尺度下，LoopMoE 在 9 个下游基准中 8 个超越传统 MoE，平均提升超 1 个点；9B 尺度下仍保持优势，表明增益可扩展。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and looped architectures scale models along two orthogonal axes, namely parameter capacity and effective depth. However, mainstream looped architectures rely on dense backbones that couple parameter count with per-token FLOPs, which makes it impossible to isolate the effect of iterative computation under matched budgets. To this end, we present LoopMoE, a looped MoE language model that integrates sparse routing with iterative weight-shared computation through two designs. The first is IterAdaLN, which resolves weight-sharing symmetry via a modulation signal jointly conditioned on the iteration index and the per-token hidden state. The second is a capacity-balancing strategy that recovers the attention-to-FFN active parameter ratio of well-tuned non-looped references. Together, these designs enable the first strictly controlled, head-to-head evaluation of a looped MoE against a Vanilla MoE under identical total parameters, per-token FLOPs, and active sublayer ratios. At the 3B scale, LoopMoE outperforms the Vanilla MoE on 8 of 9 downstream benchmarks with an average improvement exceeding 1 point. At the 9B scale, LoopMoE continues to outperform the matched Vanilla MoE, indicating that the architectural gain persists at larger scale. Our work establishes a controlled synthesis of sparsity and recurrence, and suggests a promising direction for looped language models.

</details>


### [147] [On Out-of-sample Embedding in UMAP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04451)
*Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Jason W. Fleischer*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出参数化UMAP方法，通过优化原始k近邻图中的成对交互，克服了非参数UMAP在添加新样本时的排斥效应，在高维复杂数据（如医学图像）上获得更优嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决UMAP算法无法将新样本正确嵌入到已有聚类内部（常被置于外围）的排斥效应问题。

Method: 通过优化原始k近邻图中的成对交互，并采用参数化UMAP框架进行嵌入学习。

Result: 参数化UMAP显著优于非参数化方法，尤其在复杂数据上；排斥效应在参数化UMAP中得到自然缓解。

Conclusion: 参数化UMAP通过优化近邻交互可有效克服排斥效应，产生更可靠的嵌入，适合复杂数据的降维分析。

Abstract: Neighbor embedding algorithms reveal correlations in high-dimensional data by constructing an equivalent graph representation in a lower-dimensional space. An increasingly popular algorithm is Uniform Manifold Learning and Projection (UMAP), which uses algebraic topology to map distances between the two spaces. While it works well on many types of data sets, UMAP has trouble adding out-of-sample points to a pre-existing mapping. In particular, UMAP often places new points on the periphery of the found clusters, rather than in their interiors with their correlated neighbors. Here, we overcome this ``repulsion effect'' by optimizing pairwise interactions within the original k-nearest-neighbor graph. Moreover, we show that parameterizing UMAP obtains better embeddings than non-parametric algorithms, particularly as the data gets more complex (e.g., medical images). We also show that the repulsion effect is naturally mitigated when a parameterized UMAP is employed to embed the data. We characterize different UMAP approaches using trustworthiness, nearest neighbor classifiers, and by analyzing attractive and repulsive forces in the embeddings.

</details>


### [148] [ChessMimic: Per-Rating Transformer Models for Human Move, Clock, and Outcome Prediction in Online Blitz Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04473)
*Thomas Johnson*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ChessMimic使用三个小型仅编码器Transformer模型，分别预测人类走棋、思考时间和比赛结果，在Lichess快棋数据上优于Maia系列，并开源了代码和模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有象棋AI（如Maia）在逐技能校准和细粒度预测（如思考时间、结果）方面存在不足，且参数效率不高，因此提出分Elo区间训练多个小型模型以提升校准精度和可解释性。

Method: 针对每100-Elo区间训练独立的仅编码器Transformer模型（走棋、时间、结果预测），输入包括棋局位置、历史走法、棋手等级分、计时状态，并额外训练结果和时钟模型。

Result: 走棋模型在每个Elo区间准确率超越Maia-2，9M参数模型性能介于Maia-3-5M和23M之间；结果模型AUC=0.78，优于Maia-2和逻辑回归；时钟模型Pearson r=0.41，弱于ALLIE的0.70。

Conclusion: ChessMimic通过分频带训练的多任务小型Transformer，在多个预测任务上取得具有竞争力的性能，并提供了完整的开源资源，验证了分技能校准策略的有效性。

Abstract: We present ChessMimic, a system of three small encoder-only transformers - for move, thinking-time, and outcome prediction - conditioned on the position, recent move history, player rating, and clock state. We fit a separate instance of each model per 100-Elo rating band, trading parameter efficiency for sharper per-skill calibration. On a held-out month-wide slice of Lichess Rated Blitz games ChessMimic's human move prediction accuracy outperforms Maia-2 in every Elo band. Compared to Maia-3, our 9M parameter model's accuracy sits between Maia-3-5M and Maia-3-23M without the additional complexity of Geometric Attention Bias. In addition to the move matching model, we also train a game outcome model that conditions not only on the position, but also player ratings, time control, and remaining clock times. The outcome model achieves an AUC of 0.78 out of sample, beating Maia-2 as well as logistic regressions based on material, ratings, and clock time. Finally, we train a clock model that predicts human thinking times. The clock model provides a usable but non-SOTA per-ply think-time signal under ALLIE-style filters (Pearson r = 0.41, Spearman rho = 0.50, MAE 4.10 s, against ALLIE's reported r = 0.70), with the residual gap concentrated in per-position bucket sharpness rather than bucket-marginal calibration. A public demo is at 1e4.ai and we release code, per-band weights, and the C++ data-filter pipeline code in GitHub.

</details>


### [149] [SurvPFN: Towards Foundation Models for Survival Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04564)
*Samuel Böhm,Lennart Purucker,Frank Hutter,Pascal Schlosser*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: SurvPFN 是一种基于先验数据的拟合网络，通过合成数据预训练和删失负对数似然损失，实现了在删失生存数据上的高精度预测，无需针对每个数据集调整结构或特征工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型无法处理删失数据，导致在生存预测任务中预测有偏且不准确，限制了它们在实际应用中的适用性。

Method: SurvPFN 通过三个步骤实现： (1) 使用威布尔事件时间和非信息删失机制生成合成数据； (2) 整合删失事件指示符； (3) 最小化删失负对数似然损失进行预训练。

Result: 在 SurvSet 真实世界生存任务集合上，SurvPFN 与经典和深度生存基线方法相比具有高度竞争力，且无需每个数据集单独拟合、特定生存架构或特征工程。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have made rapid progress in standard classification and regression, but time-to-event survival prediction tasks have remained largely untouched. Unlike in standard regression tasks, survival prediction models must account for censored data. Standard TFMs cannot handle natively censored data, leading to biased and inaccurate predictions, making them unsuitable for real-world applications. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose \texttt{SurvPFN}, a prior-data fitted network (PFN), for survival prediction tasks. We pretrain \texttt{SurvPFN} on millions of synthetic survival prediction tasks to learn survival via distributional regression that accounts for censored data. \texttt{SurvPFN} works by (1) generating data with Weibull event times and a non-informative censoring mechanism; (2) integrating a censored event indicator; and (3) minimizing a censored negative log-likelihood. On SurvSet, a collection of real-world survival tasks, \texttt{SurvPFN} is highly competitive with classical and deep survival baselines without per-dataset fitting, a survival-specific architecture, or feature engineering. We show that survival can be treated as a continuous-time distributional regression problem with censored loss, unlocking the power of PFNs for time-to-event predictions.

</details>


### [150] [HalfNet: Randomized Neural Networks with Learned Subspace Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04583)
*Ethem Alpaydin*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出HalfNet方法，通过学习随机权重分布的低秩协方差矩阵，在仅训练部分参数的情况下达到全参数MLP的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络预测能力主要源于权重空间的几何结构还是单个参数的具体数值。

Method: 随机权重从可学习的低秩协方差矩阵$N(0, Σ)$中采样，仅优化协方差矩阵的低秩因子。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上，HalfNet使用显著更少的参数匹配全训练MLP的性能；准确率随秩平滑提升。

Conclusion: 神经网络的预测能力主要取决于权重空间的几何结构而非精确参数值，HalfNet可解释为数据依赖的随机嵌入。

Abstract: Many researchers investigated neural networks with some of their weights fixed to values randomly drawn from a given distribution, e.g., $N(0, I)$. Our proposed HalfNet draws random weights from $N(0, Σ)$, where $Σ$, which defines the geometry of the distribution, has a low-rank factorization that we learn from data. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate that HalfNet can match the performance of fully trained multilayer perceptrons while using substantially fewer parameters. Spectral analysis indicates that much of the predictive power of neural networks lies in the geometry of their weight space rather than in the precise values of individual parameters, and we observe that accuracy scales smoothly with rank. HalfNet is not a neural architecture trick for low-rank structure; it implements a data-dependent random embedding that can also be interpreted through supervised metric learning, or random-feature and kernel perspectives.

</details>


### [151] [ALINC: Active Learning for Inductive Node Classification via Graph Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04647)
*Pascal Plettenberg,Denis Huseljic,André Alcalde,Bernhard Sick,Josephine M. Thomas*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ALINC是一个针对归纳式节点分类任务中整图选取的主动学习框架，通过聚合机制将节点级效用提升为图级选择标准，在多个数据集和策略中验证了CoreSet、TypiClust和BADGE等方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习主要针对单图节点选择，而分子化学等领域的多图归纳场景需要整图标注，缺乏相应的图级选择方法。

Method: 提出ALINC框架，利用不同聚合机制（如均值、最值等）将节点级主动学习策略（如CoreSet、TypiClust、BADGE）扩展为图级选择标准，实现批量图采样。

Result: 在十种策略、三种聚合方法和四个数据集的基准测试中，CoreSet、TypiClust和BADGE表现最佳，且聚合方法的选择显著影响性能和标注成本。

Conclusion: ALINC有效填补了归纳式节点分类中图级主动学习的方法空缺，并在分子代谢预测和电路板设计自动化等实际场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) for node classification typically focuses on selecting the most informative nodes for annotation within one or a few large graphs (e.g., in social network analysis). However, in other domains, such as molecular chemistry or electronic design automation, datasets consist of thousands of independent graphs. In many of these inductive settings, annotating an individual node requires a full-graph analysis, which effectively yields the remaining node labels on-the-fly. Therefore, these scenarios require AL strategies that select entire graphs instead of single nodes, a problem which has not been tackled in the literature so far. Thus, we introduce ALINC, an AL framework for inductive node classification via graph sampling. It bridges the existing methodological gap by elevating node-level utility measures to graph-level selection criteria through various aggregation mechanisms. In an extensive benchmark including ten strategies, three aggregation methods, and four datasets, we identify CoreSet, TypiClust, and BADGE as the top-performing graph sampling strategies. Our detailed analysis further reveals that the choice of the aggregation method is pivotal, as it substantially affects model performance and annotation costs. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ALINC in two use case studies: site-of-metabolism prediction in molecules and design automation of printed circuit board schematics.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Accurate Model Selection in Deep Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04665)
*Kaichao You,Ximei Wang,Mingsheng Long,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Deep Embedded Validation (DEV)方法，通过嵌入适应特征表示进行无偏目标风险评估，并利用控制变量技术降低方差，解决深度无监督域适应中模型选择方法存在偏差、不稳定等缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度无监督域适应中缺乏准确、标准化的模型选择方法，现有方法存在高偏差、受限、不稳定或依赖标注目标数据等问题，阻碍领域发展。

Method: 在验证过程中嵌入适应特征表示，获得目标风险的无偏估计，并通过控制变量技术进一步降低估计方差。

Result: 理论上证明无偏性和方差缩减，实验上验证方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DEV方法为深度无监督域适应提供了可靠且标准化的模型选择方案，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: Deep unsupervised domain adaptation (Deep UDA) methods successfully leverage rich labeled data in a source domain to boost the performance on related but unlabeled data in a target domain. However, algorithm comparison is cumbersome in Deep UDA due to the absence of accurate and standardized model selection method, posing an obstacle to further advances in the field. Existing model selection methods for Deep UDA are either highly biased, restricted, unstable, or even controversial (requiring labeled target data). To this end, we propose \textit{Deep Embedded Validation} (\textbf{DEV}), which embeds adapted feature representation into the validation procedure to obtain unbiased estimation of the target risk with bounded variance. The variance is further reduced by the technique of control variate. The efficacy of the method has been justified both theoretically and empirically.

</details>


### [153] [Uncertainty-Aware (Un)Supervised Few-Shot User Adaptation for On-Device Personalized Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04798)
*Maximilian Burzer,Till Riedel,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种无梯度框架，将预训练HAR分类器改造为原型网络，通过闭式贝叶斯原型估计实现轻量级个性化，在仅3秒校准数据下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR模型因领域偏移在未见用户上性能下降，需要轻量、鲁棒的个性化方法，且能适应有标签、无标签或无校准数据场景。

Method: 将预训练HAR分类器作为原型网络，使用先验原型保持零样本性能并正则化适应；对有标签校准采用闭式贝叶斯原型估计，无标签校准扩展相同原理。

Result: 在四个数据集上，监督适应宏F1提升+2.76至+33.44个百分点，无监督适应提升+0.56至+32.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过闭式原型更新实现高效鲁棒的设备端个性化，适用于已有HAR分类器。

Abstract: Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition (HAR) models often degrade on unseen users due to domain shifts caused by individual movement patterns and sensor placement. Practical wearable HAR systems therefore require personalization methods that are lightweight, applicable whether calibration data is labeled, unlabeled, or unavailable, and robust under limited calibration. We present a gradient-free framework that repurposes pretrained HAR classifiers as Prototypical Networks using using prior prototypes, which preserve zero-shot performance and regularize adaptation. For labeled calibration, we introduce closed-form Bayesian prototype estimation and extend the same principle to unlabeled calibration. With only 3 seconds of calibration data per activity (one shot), supervised adaptation improves macro-F1 by +2.76 to +33.44 percentage points across four datasets, while unsupervised adaptation improves by +0.56 to +32.13 points. Since adaptation requires only closed-form prototype updates, the framework enables efficient and robust on-device personalization of preexisting HAR classifiers.

</details>


### [154] [Provably Reduced Sample Cost in Prior-Guided Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04866)
*Leona Hennig,Jasmin Brandt,Lukas Fehring,Barbara Hammer,Marius Lindauer,Marcel Wever*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该研究首次为多保真超参数优化提供了基于先验分布的样本复杂度理论边界，证明信息性先验可显著减少评估预算（最高90%），而误导性先验不损害基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数优化方法启发式利用先验信息，但缺乏对先验信息量如何量化降低样本复杂度的理论刻画，导致计算资源浪费和可扩展性问题。

Method: 将多保真HPO形式化为固定预算的最佳臂识别问题，通过直接对臂均值（配置性能）建模先验分布，推导出与先验信息量和评估预算相关的显式分布依赖误差界。

Result: 在合成基准和LCBench深度学习基准上，信息性先验实现最多90%的预算缩减且保持解质量，无信息或误导性先验恢复基线表现。

Conclusion: 为先验引导的绿色自动化机器学习提供了理论原则，推动计算高效、可扩展的HPO方法发展。

Abstract: Large-scale hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in automated machine learning (AutoML) consumes substantial computational resources, raising growing concerns about scalability and energy efficiency. Existing methods use prior information heuristically to accelerate both black-box and multi-fidelity settings, but they lack a characterization of how prior informativeness quantitatively reduces sample complexity. In this work, we provide the first distribution-dependent sample complexity bounds for multi-fidelity HPO with priors through the formal lens of fixed-budget best-arm identification. By modeling priors directly over arm means as configuration performance, we derive explicit, distribution-dependent error bounds that quantify the relationship between priors and evaluation budget. Our analysis shows that informative priors, which concentrate probability mass on near-optimal arms, yield reductions in the number of required evaluations, whereas baseline performance is recovered with uninformative or misleading priors. We conduct proof-of-concept experiments on a synthetic benchmark and on LCBench, a common multi-fidelity HPO benchmark for deep learning, to confirm our theoretical results, achieving up to 90% budget reduction while retaining solution quality. Together, our results provide a principled foundation for prior-guided and compute-efficient green AutoML.

</details>


### [155] [Worker Utility as Hysteresis: A Preisach Model of Transaction Acceptance in Gig Labour Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04916)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 通过Preisach滞回模型和双输出神经网络，将工人二值化选择偏好转化为可估计的接受/拒绝效用曲面，在36,891条零工交易中实现Jaccard=0.827、ROC AUC=0.799，并验证了价格下降对完成率的抑制效应强于等幅上涨的促进效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零工交易中工人效用不可直接观测，仅能通过接受/拒绝二值信号推断，现有模型难以刻画工人异质性阈值分布与历史价格影响。

Method: 采用Preisach滞回模型表示工人潜在偏好，构建双输出神经网络估计接受效用U_1和拒绝效用U_0，通过边际损失强制U_1≥U_0，并将效用差与稳定化价格阈值编码输入XGBoost分类器。

Result: 价格阈值编码提升ROC AUC 11.0个百分点；工资削减21.3%的同时预期完成率提升9.7个百分点；74.2%交易可降薪且接受概率保持0.972，25.4%交易需加薪7%以提升43个百分点接受率。

Conclusion: Preisach滞回模型有效捕获工人决策的方向性不对称性，且引入明确无差异区的模型可同步实施降薪与加薪策略，优于无此机制的传统模型。

Abstract: Worker utility is not observed -- only its consequence is. Each gig transaction produces a single bit: accepted or rejected. We argue this structure points directly to the Preisach hysteresis model as the natural representation of latent worker preferences. The Preisach operator models aggregate output as an integral over a population of binary threshold elements -- precisely the structure that emerges when heterogeneous workers each carry a private acceptance wage. We estimate two latent utility surfaces: acceptance utility U_1(X) and rejection utility U_0(X), via a dual-output neural network (shared layers 256->128, margin loss enforcing U_1 >= U_0). Classification reduces to the Preisach gap U_1(X) - U_0(X), passed into an XGBoost classifier alongside clip-stabilised price-to-threshold encodings. On 36,891 gig transactions, this pipeline achieves Jaccard = 0.827 and ROC AUC = 0.799. The price-to-threshold encoding accounts for +11.0 pp AUC over raw utility features. The model confirms the directional asymmetry hysteresis predicts: price decreases depress completion rates more than equivalent increases raise them. Applied to the full dataset, the model's recommendations simultaneously reduce the total wage bill by 21.3% and increase expected fill rate by 9.7 pp. For 74.2% of transactions, P(accept) already exceeds 0.80; reducing the wage keeps it above threshold (mean post-cut P = 0.972), releasing cost savings (median 31%). For the remaining 25.4%, a median 7% wage increase recovers +43 pp acceptance. A model without an explicit indifference zone cannot execute both moves simultaneously.

</details>


### [156] [Mean-based algorithms: A lower bound and regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04931)
*Julius Durmann,Amelie Kleber*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 在未知时间范围的bandit反馈下，均值算法存在学习速度下限，但可设计出同时具备均值特性与无遗憾特性的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前均值算法在bandit反馈中收敛速度慢于经典算法，且缺乏理论下限分析；需明确其性能边界并探索与无遗憾算法的兼容性。

Method: 推导了算法定义序列γ_t的下界，提出两种适应未知时间范围的均值算法（泛化ε-greedy和扩展Exp3），并通过实验对比其他bandit算法。

Result: 均值算法虽略慢但竞争力强；证明了通过调整γ_t可同时满足均值性和无遗憾性。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mean-based algorithms are a class of online learning algorithms that assign low probability to actions with low average rewards. Recent work indicates these algorithms converge favorably to serially undominated actions, which approximate Nash equilibria in economic games. However, empirical studies also show slower convergence compared to established algorithms in bandit-feedback scenarios.
  We study mean-based algorithms when the time horizon is unknown and only bandit feedback is available. In this setting, we provide the first lower bound on the algorithm-defining sequence $γ_t$ that formally establishes a limit on how fast these algorithms can learn. Additionally, we propose two mean-based algorithms: one generalizes $ε$-greedy, and the other extends the mean-based Exp3 to unknown horizons. Our experiments show that mean-based algorithms, although slightly slower, can perform competitively with other bandit-feedback algorithms.
  We further analyze the relationship to no-regret algorithms. Depending on the choice of $γ_t$, the intersection with no-regret algorithms is non-trivial, and we show that algorithms exist that are both mean-based and no-regret. This adds context to the "exploitability" of this class of algorithms that previous contributions suggest.

</details>


### [157] [New Benchmarking Shows Limited Generalization Power of TCR Antigenic Epitope Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04994)
*Yiming Liao,Yiheng Li,Ning Jiang,Bo Li,Keke Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 现有TCR-抗原特异性预测模型因缺乏严格定义的未见基准数据集而性能不足，本文提出两类互补数据集以建立鲁棒评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型在灵敏度和特异性上无法满足广泛应用，主要瓶颈是缺少无偏的基准数据集来评估性能与泛化能力。

Method: 通过构建两类互补的未见基准数据集，提供模型评估框架和下一代算法开发基础。

Result: 提出的数据集满足严格基准标准，能实现无偏性能评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集框架可推动TCR-抗原特异性预测算法的进步，助力T细胞生物学研究与免疫工程。

Abstract: Accurate computational prediction of T cell receptor (TCR) antigen specificity would transform the study of T cell biology and enable scalable immune engineering, yet existing models lack sufficient sensitivity and specificity for broad applications. A major limitation is the absence of rigorously defined, unseen benchmark datasets that allow unbiased evaluation of model performance and generalizability. Here, we describe two complementary classes of datasets that meet this criterion and argue that they provide both a robust framework for model assessment and a foundation for next-generation TCR-antigen prediction algorithm development.

</details>


### [158] [FLAGG: Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05067)
*Samuel Cognolato,Alessandro Sperduti,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FLAGG框架，通过顺序生成图的部分并使用一次性模型，结合两者的优势，在多种图数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一次性模型和顺序模型各有所长，但均无法适用于所有图类型（如一次性模型生成大图困难，顺序模型在小图上表现不佳），因此需要灵活组合两者。

Method: FLAGG框架：使用一次性模型（如DiGress）以自回归方式顺序生成图的局部，顺序策略由随机节点移除过程定义，并通过插入模型学习逆转该过程。

Result: 在多个不同规模和领域的图数据集上，FLAGG在采样质量上优于一次性模型和自回归基线方法。

Conclusion: FLAGG框架通过灵活结合一次性与顺序生成方法，克服了各自的局限性，提升了对不同图类型的适用性和生成质量。

Abstract: The Deep Graph Generation's panorama spans two extremes: one-shot and sequential models. The former generates nodes and edges jointly, while the latter samples them autoregressively. Each method performs better in different graph domains depending on size and topology, but neither is applicable to all graph categories. For instance, one-shot methods struggle with generating large graphs, while sequential methods underperform on smaller graphs. A possible way to overcome these limitations is to flexibly combine the two methods in a unique system. In this work, we propose the FLAGG (Flexible Autoregressive Graph Generation) framework, which sequentially generates portions of graphs with one-shot models. FLAGG can apply any one-shot model to make it autoregressive, allowing flexibility in choosing the sequential policy. This policy is specified through a stochastic node removal process, which an Insertion Model learns to reverse. We evaluate FLAGG with the DiGress one-shot model on several data sets of different graph sizes and domains. We show that the approach outperforms both one-shot and autoregressive baselines in terms of sampling quality.

</details>


### [159] [Learning What Not to Impute: An Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Framework for Meaningful Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05073)
*Lixing Zhang,Yidong Ouyang,Weifu Li,Shixiang Zhu,Guang Cheng,Liyan Xie*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Diff-Joint框架，联合建模表格数据与潜在缺失模式，区分有意义的缺失与需填补的缺失，在合成与真实数据集上实现精准识别与高质量填补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失值填补方法均假设所有缺失为随机缺失（需填补），但实际数据中缺失可能来自两种不同源头：有意义的缺失（本质不应存在）与观察过程导致的缺失（需恢复）。

Method: 提出Diff-Joint扩散模型，联合建模表格数据与隐式缺失掩码，通过迭代条件采样与不确定性感知聚合，交替优化填补值与缺失标签。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，Diff-Joint有效识别有意义的缺失值，同时保持竞争力的填补精度并提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: Diff-Joint成功将缺失值填补从统一处理拓展为选择性填补，为处理双重缺失机制的数据提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Missing value imputation is a fundamental task in machine learning, with most existing methods assuming that all missing entries correspond to unobserved regular values. In many real-world datasets, however, missingness may arise from two distinct sources: some entries are meaningfully missing (intrinsically absent and semantically valid), while others are missing due to the observation process and should be imputed. We formalize this distinction as a selective imputation problem, where the goal is to jointly infer which missing entries should be preserved and which should be recovered. To address this challenge, we propose Diff-Joint, a diffusion-based framework that jointly models tabular data together with a latent missingness mask. The method alternates between conditional sampling and uncertainty-aware aggregation to iteratively refine both imputed values and missingness labels. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that Diff-Joint effectively identifies meaningfully missing entries while achieving competitive imputation accuracy and improved downstream task performance.

</details>


### [160] [BBOmix: A Tabular Benchmark for Hyperparameter Optimization of Unsupervised Biological Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05139)
*Luca Thale-Bombien,Jan Ewald,Ralf König,Aaron Klein*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了BBOmix基准，用于评估无监督表示学习中自编码器的超参数优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自编码器对架构和超参数敏感，且重构损失可能无法反映下游任务性能，而全面超参数优化计算成本高，现有研究常依赖次优默认配置。

Method: 构建包含105,000次评估的BBOmix基准，覆盖四种自编码器架构和七个多组学数据集，量化重构损失与下游任务性能的相关性，并评估多种超参数优化方法。

Result: 提供了单保真度、多保真度和迁移学习超参数优化方法的严格基线，揭示了重构损失与下游性能的关系。

Conclusion: BBOmix基准可促进无监督生物学表示学习中的超参数优化研究，降低大规模实验门槛。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high-throughput sequencing has led to large, high-dimensional omics datasets. Deep unsupervised learning architectures, particularly Autoencoders (AEs), are increasingly used for dimensionality reduction and representation learning in this domain. However, AEs are highly sensitive to architectural choices and hyperparameters, and unsupervised optimization typically relies on reconstruction loss, which may be a poor proxy for downstream utility. Exhaustive hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is computationally expensive, leading researchers to frequently rely on suboptimal default configurations. To democratize access to large-scale unsupervised HPO research, we introduce $\textbf{BBOmix}$, the first open-source tabular benchmark for unsupervised representation learning on real-world biological data. Our benchmark includes 105,000 evaluations across four AE architectures and seven multi-omics modalities from the TCGA and SCHC datasets. We quantify the correlation between reconstruction loss and downstream task performance and provide an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art single-fidelity, multi-fidelity, and transfer learning HPO methods, establishing a rigorous baseline for future research in unsupervised biological representation learning.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [MaskForge: Structure-Aware Adaptive Attacks for Jailbreaking Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04027)
*Yingzi Ma,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Minhui Xue,Yue Zhao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: MaskForge是一种针对扩散大语言模型的黑盒自适应越狱攻击方法，通过可重用模式库和UCB bandit选择策略，在五个模型和三个基准上平均攻击成功率达79.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱方法要么忽略了扩散LLM的原生填充能力，要么依赖低多样性的掩码模板，缺乏结构自适应性和攻击经验积累。

Method: MaskForge将越狱建模为对结构模式库的优化搜索，通过UCB bandit选择与目标兼容的模式，并在失败时启用评分器引导的回退方案，成功攻击经验被蒸馏回模式库以持续积累。

Result: 在五个公开扩散LLM和三个基准上平均攻击成功率达79.3%，比最强基线相对提升17.6%；模式库可迁移至AdvBench，攻击成功率88.2%，相对提升67%。

Conclusion: MaskForge证明了通过自适应模式库和攻击经验积累，能有效利用扩散LLM的填充能力实现高效越狱，且模式库具有良好的跨目标迁移性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising partially masked sequences under bidirectional context, exposing a safety surface distinct from autoregressive LLMs. Because mask tokens are native inputs and tokens are committed by confidence rather than position, harmful content can be induced through infilling and outside the monitored prefix. Existing jailbreaks either miss this native infill capability or rely on low-diversity mask-bearing templates applied uniformly across goals, with little structural adaptation or accumulated attack experience. We propose MaskForge, a fully black-box adaptive attack that casts dLLM red-teaming as optimized search over a growing library of structural patterns. MaskForge abstracts successful attempts into reusable schemas, selects goal-compatible patterns with a UCB bandit, and invokes a scorer-guided fallback when the current library fails. Successful attempts are distilled back into the pattern library, enabling experience to accumulate across goals. Across five public dLLMs and three benchmarks, MaskForge achieves an average attack success rate of 79.3%, a 17.6% relative improvement over the strongest competing dLLM baseline. The matured pattern library further transfers to AdvBench without any updates, achieving a 88.2% attack success rate and a 67% relative improvement over the strongest competing baseline.

</details>


### [162] [Bayesian Membership Privacy for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04069)
*Sinan Yıldırım,Megha Khosla*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯成员隐私（BMP），一种考虑图采样概率和节点先验的节点级成员隐私定义，并通过贝叶斯假设检验量化隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN隐私分析忽略结构相关性和随机训练图采样，仅用第一类/第二类错误无法刻画最优成员推理测试。

Method: 将成员推理建模为贝叶斯假设检验，引入节点依赖先验和采样概率作为攻击者知识，定义后验成员概率作为隐私度量，并设计采样感知审计机制估计参数。

Result: 在基准图数据集上实验表明，BMP能提供全局攻击准确率无法揭示的细粒度隐私洞察。

Conclusion: BMP是一种更精确的GNN节点级成员隐私度量，能够捕捉采样和结构依赖性带来的隐私差异。

Abstract: Existing privacy analyses for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) largely inherit assumptions from non-graph settings, overlooking structural correlations and stochastic training-graph sampling. In particular, node-dependent priors make type-I and type-II errors alone insufficient to characterize the best membership inference test. To address this, we introduce Bayesian Membership Privacy (BMP), a sampling-aware formulation of node-level membership privacy that incorporates node-dependent priors and treats graph sampling probabilities as part of the adversary's knowledge. BMP casts membership inference as a Bayesian hypothesis test and accordingly quantifies membership privacy in terms of posterior membership probability. We explore theoretical properties of BMP in relation to the existing definitions in the literature. We further propose a practical, sampling-aware auditing mechanism to estimate the parameters of BMP as a measure of node-level privacy leakage in GNNs. We conduct experiments on benchmark graph datasets and show that BMP yields fine-grained privacy insights that are not visible through global attack accuracy alone.

</details>


### [163] [Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)
*Thanh Luong Tuan,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于本体的AI Agent预部署验证框架，包括操作包络、场景生成和信任证书，在四类受监管行业的实验表明本体驱动可提升监管覆盖率但优势有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI Agent在部署前的验证存在空白，现有后置监控、人工控制和提示级防护在生成环境中保障不足。

Method: 构建本体驱动的验证框架：Agent操作包络（定义权限、领域约束、安全属性、治理规则和自主级别）、本体到场景的生成管道（自动推导监管、操作和对抗场景）、可信证书（可机器验证的渐进式部署判定）。

Result: 在四个受监管行业（美国/越南）的500个设置中，本体驱动方法（G4）监管覆盖率48.3%高于基线33.1%（p=0.0006），领域特异性最高（4.77/5.0, p=2e-6），但经Bonferroni校正后优势不稳健；三个LLM家族的交叉验证复制了该模式。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production. We propose an ontology-grounded verification framework combining three components: an Agent Operational Envelope formalizing the certification space across permissions, domain constraints, safety properties, governance rules, and autonomy levels; an ontology-to-scenario generation pipeline that derives regulatory, operational, and adversarial test scenarios automatically; and a Trust Certificate carrying a machine-verifiable attestation with graduated deployment verdicts (Approved, Conditional, Rejected). A controlled pilot across four regulated industries (Fintech, Banking, Insurance, and Healthcare), instantiated as five industry-by-regulatory-regime cells across the United States and Vietnam, generated 1,800 scenarios evaluated against 125 primary-source regulatory requirements and 25 injected faults. Ontology-grounded generation (G4) achieved 48.3% regulatory coverage versus 33.1% for the persona-based baseline (corrected p = .0006) and the highest domain specificity (4.77/5.0; p = 2e-6). The coverage advantage over baseline and retrieval-augmented prompting was not robust after Bonferroni correction. Cross-validation across three LLM families (Claude Sonnet 4, Qwen 2.5 72B, Gemma 4 26B; 5,400 total scenarios) replicated the persona-versus-ontology pattern. The results establish ontology-grounded scenario generation as a credible complement to persona-based test suites for regulatory-intensive domains.

</details>


### [164] [Caught in the Act(ivation): Toward Pre-Output and Multi-Turn Detection of Credential Exfiltration by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04141)
*Kargi Chauhan,Pratibha Revankar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 研究了LLM代理中凭证泄露的三种防御方法：激活探针预检测、蜜令令牌校准和多轮泄露累积追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理将敏感凭证与不可信内容置于同一上下文窗口，导致间接提示注入攻击可窃取凭证。

Method: 使用激活探针在输出前检测凭证访问；构建基于格式字符模型的蜜令令牌并采用分裂共形预测校准；将多轮泄露建模为累积信息流问题并追踪预算。

Result: 激活特征高精度区分良性与凭证窃取提示；累积账户法检测到单轮检测遗漏的攻击。

Conclusion: 凭证泄露防御应结合输出前监控、校准蜜令检测和时间泄露账户，而非仅依赖文本级输出过滤器。

Abstract: LLM agents often place sensitive credentials in the same context window as untrusted retrieved content, creating a direct path for indirect prompt injection to induce credential exfiltration. We study this failure mode through three complementary defenses. First, we ask whether activation probes can detect credential access before output tokens are emitted. Second, we construct honeytokens from format-specific character models and calibrate detection with split conformal prediction. Third, we treat multi-turn exfiltration as a cumulative information-flow problem and track an estimated leakage budget across conversation turns. In controlled experiments on open-weight models, activation features separate benign and credential-seeking prompts with high accuracy, including under held-out encoding transformations. In a small synthetic multi-turn suite, cumulative accounting detects attacks that per-turn detectors miss. These results are preliminary: the multi-turn benchmark is in-house and small, the activation method requires white-box access, and the information estimator provides a practical signal rather than a formal upper bound. Still, the results suggest that credential-exfiltration defenses should combine pre-output monitoring, calibrated canary detection, and temporal leakage accounting rather than relying only on text-level output filters.

</details>


### [165] [Notarized Agents: Receiver-Attested Confidential Receipts for AI Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04193)
*Juan Figuera*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Sello协议，通过服务端签名、HPKE加密、公钥日志发布和令牌引用发现，实现AI代理活动日志的独立可验证性，解决自记录代理的篡改风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理可观测性存在结构性缺陷：记录日志的实体与被记录的代理为同一实体，受损或恶意代理可随意篡改、伪造日志，而操作者无法独立检测。

Method: 设计协议类及其实例Sello，包含四个特性：接收端签名(P1)、通过JWS绑定所有者公钥的HPKE加密(P2)、发布至见证联签的Merkle树日志(P3)、基于令牌引用的所有者侧发现(P4)。

Result: 通过安全分析证明在代理及操作者被控制场景下日志的防篡改性，给出密码操作微基准测试，并与Signet等现有协议对比。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Current AI agent observability is structurally compromised: the entity producing the activity log is the same entity whose activity is being logged. A compromised or buggy agent can omit, alter, or fabricate its own traces, and the operator running the agent has no independent way to detect tampering. We propose a class of protocols that resolves this by inverting the trust boundary: the service that receives an agent's call signs a receipt of what it observed using its own key, encrypts the receipt to the agent's owner, and publishes it to a public transparency log. The owner reconstructs a tamper-evident trail without trusting the agent or its operator. We instantiate the class as Sello, a protocol combining four properties absent in any current system: (P1) receiver-side signing, (P2) HPKE encryption to an owner public key bound to the authorization token via JWS, (P3) publication to a witness-cosigned Merkle log, and (P4) owner-side discovery by token reference. We describe the protocol, analyze its security under an adversary that controls the agent and its operator, present microbenchmarks of the cryptographic operations, and situate Sello among adjacent receipt-protocol work (Signet, AgentROA, Agent Passport System, draft-farley-acta, SCITT). We discuss known limitations including the suppression attack, service collusion, and the adoption-incentive problem.

</details>


### [166] [Pseudospectral Bounds for Transient Amplification in Coupled Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04031)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 针对耦合梯度下降中块三角雅可比矩阵的非正态性导致的暂态放大问题，提出了尖锐的伪谱理论，证明了Kreiss常数的上界并给出匹配极小极大下界，进一步推导了随机耦合下降的有限步迭代复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
